Poll every {}s.\",\n                          pollInterval_.count());\n    }\n\n    void stop() noexcept\n    {\n        {\n            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(startStopMutex_);\n            if (!workerThread_.joinable())\n                return; // Not running\n            stopFlag_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);\n        }\n        cv_.notify_all();\n        workerThread_.join();\n        log::Logger::info(\"[SchedPub] ScheduledPublicationService stopped.\");\n    }\n\n    bool isRunning() const noexcept { return workerThread_.joinable();   // No newlines\n    BIO* bio = BIO_new(BIO_s_mem());\n    BIO_push(b64, bio);\n\n    BIO_write(b64, data.data(), static_cast<int>(data.size()));\n    BIO_flush(b64);\n\n    BUF_MEM* bufferPtr = nullptr;\n    BIO_get_mem_ptr(b64, &bufferPtr);\n\n    std::string encoded(bufferPtr->data, bufferPtr->length);\n    BIO_free_all(b64);\n\n    // Standard -> URL safe\n    for (char& c : encoded)\n    {\n        if (c == '+') c = '-';\n        else if (c == '/') c = '_';\n    }\n\n    // Trim padding '='\n    while (!encoded.empty() && encoded.back() == '=')\n        encoded.pop_back();\n\n    return encoded;\n}\n\ninline std::vector<uint8_t> base64url_decode(std::string_view input)\n{\n    std::string temp(input);\n\n    // URL safe -> standard\n    for (char& c : temp)\n    {\n        if (c == '-') c = '+';\n        else if (c == '_') c = '/';\n    }\n\n    // Restore padding if necessary\n    while (temp.size() % 4 != 0)\n        temp.push_back('=');\n\n    BIO* b64 = BIO_new(BIO_f_base64());\n    BIO_set_flags(b64, BIO_FLAGS_BASE64_NO_NL);\n    BIO* bio = BIO_new_mem_buf(temp.data(), static_cast<int>(temp.size()));\n    bio    = BIO_push(b64, bio);\n\n    std::vector<uint8_t> decoded(temp.size());\n    const int len = BIO_read(bio, decoded.data(),\n                             static_cast<int>(decoded.size()));\n    if (len <= 0)\n    {\n        BIO_free_all(bio);\n        throw TokenInvalid(\"Base64 decode failed\");\n    }\n    decoded.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(len));\n    BIO_free_all(bio);\n    return decoded;\n}\n} // namespace detail\n\n/*--------------------------------------------------------\n * SecureTokenService\n *-------------------------------------------------------*/\nclass SecureTokenService\n{\npublic:\n    struct Config\n    {\n        std::string       secretKey;     // HMAC secret (binary safe)\n        Seconds           defaultTTL     = Seconds { 900 }; // 15 minutes\n        std::string       issuer         = \"IntraLedger-BlogSuite\";\n        std::size_t       randomBytes    = 16;  // Random nonce length\n    };\n\n    explicit SecureTokenService(Config config)\n        : cfg_(std::move(config))\n    {\n        if (cfg_.secretKey.empty())\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"Secret key must not be empty\");\n    }\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * generateToken\n     *\n     * Generates a compact, URL-safe token for an arbitrary\n     * user payload.  The payload is augmented with standard\n     * claims (iss, iat, exp, jti).\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    std::string generateToken(Json userPayload,\n                              std::optional<Seconds> ttl = std::nullopt) const\n    {\n        const auto now = Clock::now();\n        const auto exp = now + (ttl ? *ttl : cfg_.defaultTTL);\n\n        userPayload[\"iss\"] = cfg_.issuer;\n        userPayload[\"iat\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 now.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"exp\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 exp.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"jti\"] = randomHex(cfg_.randomBytes);\n\n        const std::string payloadStr = userPayload.dump();\n        const std::vector<uint8_t> payloadBin(\n            payloadStr.begin(), payloadStr.end());\n\n        const std::string encodedPayload =\n            detail::base64url_encode(payloadBin);\n\n        const std::vector<uint8_t> signature =\n            hmacSHA256(encodedPayload);\n\n        const std::string encodedSig = detail::base64url_encode(signature);\n\n        return encodedPayload + '.' + encodedSig;\n    }\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * verifyToken\n     *\n     * Checks integrity & validity, then returns the decoded\n     * payload on success.  On failure, a TokenError\n     * derivative is thrown.\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    Json verifyToken(std::string_view token) const\n    {\n        const auto delimPos = token.find('.');\n        if (delimPos == std::string_view::npos)\n            throw TokenInvalid(\"Token format incorrect\");\n\n        const std::string_view encodedPayload = token.substr(0, delimPos);\n        const std::string_view encodedSig     =\n            token.substr(delimPos + 1);\n\n        // Verify signature\n        const std::vector<uint8_t> expectedSig =\n            hmacSHA256(encodedPayload);\n\n        const std::vector<uint8_t> providedSig =\n            detail::base64url_decode(encodedSig);\n\n        if (!constantTimeEquals(expectedSig, providedSig))\n            throw TokenInvalid(\"Signature mismatch\");\n\n        // Decode payload\n        const std::vector<uint8_t> payloadBin =\n            detail::base64url_decode(encodedPayload);\n        const std::string payloadStr(payloadBin.begin(), payloadBin.end());\n\n        Json payload;\n        try\n        {\n            payload = Json::parse(payloadStr);\n        }\n        catch (const Json::exception& e)\n        {\n            throw TokenInvalid(\"Malformed JSON payload\");\n        }\n\n        // Validate timestamps\n        const auto nowSecs =\n            std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                Clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();\n\n        if (payload.contains(\"exp\") && payload[\"exp\"].is_number())\n        {\n            if (nowSecs > payload[\"exp\"].get<int64_t>())\n                throw TokenExpired();\n        }\n\n        if (payload.contains(\"nbf\") && payload[\"nbf\"].is_number())\n        {\n            if (nowSecs < payload[\"nbf\"].get<int64_t>())\n                throw TokenInvalid(\"Token not yet valid (nbf)\");\n        }\n\n        return payload;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    Config cfg_;\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * hmacSHA256\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    std::vector<uint8_t> hmacSHA256(std::string_view data) const\n    {\n        unsigned int len = EVP_MAX_MD_SIZE;\n        std::vector<uint8_t> result(len);\n\n        HMAC_CTX* ctx = HMAC_CTX_new();\n        if (!ctx)\n            throw TokenError(\"HMAC_CTX allocation failed\");\n\n        if (HMAC_Init_ex(ctx,\n                         cfg_.secretKey.data(),\n                         static_cast<int>(cfg_.secretKey.size()),\n                         EVP_sha256(), nullptr) != 1)\n        {\n            HMAC_CTX_free(ctx);\n            throw TokenError(\"HMAC_Init_ex failed\");\n        }\n\n        HMAC_Update(ctx,\n                    reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(data.data()),\n                    data.size());\n\n        HMAC_Final(ctx, result.data(), &len);\n        HMAC_CTX_free(ctx);\n        result.resize(len);\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * randomHex\n     *\n     * Generates a cryptographically secure random hex\n     * string of length `byteCount * 2`.\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    static std::string randomHex(std::size_t byteCount)\n    {\n        std::vector<uint8_t> buf(byteCount);\n        if (RAND_bytes(buf.data(), static_cast<int>(buf.size())) != 1)\n            throw TokenError(\"RAND_bytes failed\");\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        oss << std::hex << std::setfill('0');\n        for (uint8_t b : buf)\n            oss << std::setw(2) << static_cast<int>(b);\n        return oss.str();\n    }\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * constantTimeEquals\n     *\n     * Compares two byte sequences in constant time to\n     * mitigate timing attacks.\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    static bool constantTimeEquals(std::span<const uint8_t> a,\n                                   std::span<const uint8_t> b)\n    {\n        if (a.size() != b.size())\n            return false;\n        uint8_t diff = 0;\n        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i)\n            diff |= a[i] ^ b[i];\n        return diff == 0;\n    }\n}; // class SecureTokenService\n\n/*--------------------------------------------------------\n * Unit-test like self-check (only compiled in debug builds)\n *-------------------------------------------------------*/\n#ifndef NDEBUG\n#include <cassert>\nstatic void self_check()\n{\n    using namespace std::chrono_literals;\n    intraledger::security::SecureTokenService::Config cfg;\n    cfg.secretKey = \"super-secret-server-key!\";\n    intraledger::security::SecureTokenService svc(cfg);\n\n    nlohmann::json payload = {\n        { \"uid\", 42 },\n        { \"role\", \"admin\" }\n    };\n\n    const std::string token = svc.generateToken(payload, 10s);\n    spdlog::debug(\"Generated token: {}\", token);\n\n    const auto decoded = svc.verifyToken(token);\n    assert(decoded[\"uid\"] == 42);\n    assert(decoded[\"role\"] == \"admin\");\n}\nstatic const int _ = (self_check(), 0);\n#endif\n\n} // namespace intraledger::security\n```","numLines":360,"startLine":1,"totalLines":360}}}

It follows the classic\n *  “voter” strategy found in many security frameworks (e.g., Spring Security)\n *  and adds a thread-safe, bounded LRU cache to avoid re-computing expensive\n *  decisions for k, Value& out_value) {\n        std::shared_lock lock(mu_);\n        auto it = map_.find(k);\n        if (it == map_.end()) return false;\n        // Move touched item to front\n        {\n map_.erase(last->first);\n            list_.pop_back();\n        }\n        list_.emplace_front(k, std::move(v));\n        map_[k] = list_.begin();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    mutable std::shared_mutex\n    if (lower == \"not\")\n   187→                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n   188→\n   189→            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n   190→        }\n   191→\n   192→        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n   193→    }\n   194→\n   195→    Token lex_phrase()\n   196→    {\n   197→        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n   198→        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n   199→\n   200→        std::ostringstream oss;\n   201→        bool escaped = false;\n   202→\n   203→        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n   204→        {\n   205→            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n   206→            if (escaped)\n   207→            {\n   208→                // start_pos };\n   227→            }\n   228→            else\n   229→            {\n   230→                oss << ch;\n   231→            }\n   232→        }\n   233→\n   234→        // Reached sv)\n   239→    {\n   240→        std::string out;\n   241→        out.reserve(sv.size());\n   242→        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n   243→                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n   244→        return out;\n   245→    }\n   246→};\n   247→\n   248→// 251→\n   252→struct QueryNode;\n   253→\n   254→using NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n   255→\n   256→struct Term\n   257→{\n   258→    std::string value;\n   259→};\n   260→\n   261→struct 362→    // Unary: NOT\n   363→    NodePtr parse_unary()\n   364→    {\n   365→        if (match(TokenType::Not))\n   366→        {\n   367→            auto arg = parse_unary();\n   368→ Tokenising raw, user-supplied query strings\n *                 2. Building an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST)\n *                 3. Translating the AST into a SQL fragment that can be\n *                    embedded safely (with positional parameters) in queries\n *                    issued by our ORM layer.\n *\n *               All public APIs are contained under namespace:\n *                   IntraLedger::Search\n *\n *               The code follows modern C++20 best-practices, makes heavy use\n *               of RAII, ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Lexer\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nenum class TokenType\n{\n m_current { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", 0 };\n\n    // Skip whitespace but keep track of cursor\n    void skip_ws()\n    {\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n    }\n\n    // Read characters while predicate holds\n    template <typename Pred>\n    std::string std::string { m_input.substr(start, m_cursor - start) };\n    }\n\n    Token lex()\n    {\n        skip_ws();\n        if (m_cursor >= m_input.size())\n            return { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", m_cursor };\n\n        const char ch = m_input[m_cursor];\n\n        // Punctuation tokens\n        switch (ch)\n        {\n            case ':':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::Colon, \":\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '(':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::LParen, \"(\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case ')':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::RParen, \")\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '\"':\n                return lex_phrase();\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        // Word or reserved keyword\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch)) || ch == '_' || ch == '-')\n        {\n            auto word = read_while([](unsigned char c) {\n                return std::isalnum(c) || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '.';\n            });\n\n            const auto lower = to_lower(word);\n            if (lower == \"and\")\n                return { TokenType::And, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"or\")\n                return { TokenType::Or, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"not\")\n                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n\n            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n    }\n\n    Token lex_phrase()\n    {\n        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        bool escaped = false;\n\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n        {\n            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n            if (escaped)\n            {\n                // Accept common escape sequences or keep char as is\n                switch (ch)\n                {\n                    case '\"':  oss << '\"'; break;\n                    case '\\\\': oss << '\\\\'; break;\n                    case 'n':  oss << '\\n'; break;\n                    case 't':  oss << '\\t'; break;\n                    default:   oss << ch;  break;\n                }\n                escaped = false;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\\\\')\n            {\n                escaped = true;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\"')\n            {\n                // End of phrase\n                return { TokenType::Phrase, oss.str(), start_pos };\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                oss << ch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Reached end-of-input without closing quote\n        throw ParseError { \"Unterminated string literal\", start_pos };\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  AST definitions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct QueryNode;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n\nstruct Term\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct Phrase\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct FieldFilter\n{\n    std::string field;  // e.g. \"title\"\n    NodePtr     arg;    // Term or Phrase\n};\n\nstruct Not\n{\n    NodePtr arg;\n};\n\nstruct And\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct Or\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct QueryNode : std::variant<Term, Phrase, FieldFilter, Not, And, Or>\n{\n    using variant::variant;\n\n    // Helper predicates\n    bool is_logical() const\n    {\n        return std::holds_alternative<And>(*this) || std::holds_alternative<Or>(*this)\n               || std::holds_alternative<Not>(*this);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Parser (recursive-descent)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass Parser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Parser(std::string_view input) : m_lexer(input)\n    {\n        m_lexer.reset(); // initialise first token\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse()\n    {\n        auto expr = parse_or();\n        expect(TokenType::EndOfInput);\n        return expr;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    Lexer m_lexer;\n\n    // Highest level: OR\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_and();\n        while (match(TokenType::Or))\n        {\n            auto rhs = parse_and();\n            node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Or { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n        }\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Intermediate level: AND (implicit or explicit)\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_unary();\n\n        while (true)\n        {\n            if (match(TokenType::And))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            // Implicit AND: two consecutive terms / groups\n            else if (peek_any({ TokenType::Word, TokenType::Phrase, TokenType::LParen, TokenType::Not }))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Unary: NOT\n    NodePtr parse_unary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::Not))\n        {\n            auto arg = parse_unary();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Not { std::move(arg) });\n        }\n        return parse_primary();\n    }\n\n    // Primary: term, phrase, parenthesis, field filter\n    NodePtr parse_primary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::LParen))\n        {\n            auto inner = parse_or();\n            expect(TokenType::RParen);\n            return inner;\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n        {\n            auto wordTok = m_lexer.next();\n\n            // Field filter?\n            if (match(TokenType::Colon))\n            {\n                const std::string fieldName = to_lower(wordTok.lexeme);\n\n                // Support only whitelisted fields\n                if (!is_whitelisted_field(fieldName))\n                    throw ParseError { \"Unknown filter field '\" + fieldName + \"'\", wordTok.position };\n\n                // Value can be phrase or term\n                NodePtr valueNode;\n                if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n                {\n                    auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode      = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n                {\n                    auto termTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode    = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { termTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    throw ParseError { \"Missing value after field filter ':'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n                }\n\n                return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(FieldFilter { fieldName, std::move(valueNode) });\n            }\n\n            /* Plain term */\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { wordTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n        {\n            auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { \"Unexpected token '\" + m_lexer.peek().lexeme + \"'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    // Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    bool match(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (peek(t))\n        {\n            m_lexer.consume();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    void expect(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (!match(t))\n            throw ParseError { \"Expected different token\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    bool peek(TokenType t) const { return m_lexer.peek().type == t; }\n\n    bool peek_any(std::initializer_list<TokenType> list) const\n    {\n        const TokenType cur = m_lexer.peek().type;\n        return std::find(list.begin(), list.end(), cur) != list.end();\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    static bool is_whitelisted_field(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        static constexpr std::array allowed = { \"title\", \"body\", \"tag\", \"author\" };\n        return std::find(allowed.begin(), allowed.end(), field) != allowed.end();\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  SQL Translation\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct SqlFragment\n{\n    std::string              sql;     // WHERE clause without the \"WHERE\" keyword\n    std::vector<std::string> params;  // positional parameters to bind in order\n};\n\nclass SqlTranslator\n{\npublic:\n    // Compile AST into SQL WHERE fragment\n    SqlFragment compile(const QueryNode& root)\n    {\n        m_buffer.clear();\n        m_params.clear();\n        walk(root);\n        return { m_buffer.str(), m_params };\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::ostringstream        m_buffer;\n    std::vector<std::string>  m_params;\n\n    // Depth-first traversal\n    void walk(const QueryNode& node)\n    {\n        std::visit([this](auto&& n) { generate(n); }, node);\n    }\n\n    // Generates SQL with PostgreSQL/MariaDB full-text search in mind.\n    // The ORM will later splice this fragment into a larger statement.\n    void generate(const Term& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Phrase& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery_phrase(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const FieldFilter& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        const std::string columnName = map_field_to_column(n.field);\n        m_buffer << columnName << \" @@ \";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Not& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"NOT (\";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const And& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" AND \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Or& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" OR \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    // Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery(const std::string& term)\n    {\n        // Simplistic sanitisation; production code would do stemming, etc.\n        std::string sanitized;\n        sanitized.reserve(term.size());\n        for (char c : term)\n        {\n            if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) || c == '_')\n                sanitized.push_back(static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)));\n        }\n        return sanitized + \":*\"; // prefix search\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery_phrase(const std::string& phrase)\n    {\n        // Replace spaces with & to enforce term adjacency in tsquery\n        std::string ts = phrase;\n        std::replace(ts.begin(), ts.end(), ' ', '&');\n        return ts;\n    }\n\n    static std::string map_field_to_column(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        if (field == \"title\")  return \"search_title\";\n        if (field == \"body\")   return \"search_body\";\n        if (field == \"tag\")    return \"search_tags\";\n        if (field == \"author\") return \"search_author\";\n        // Fallback should never happen thanks to whitelist in parser\n        throw std::logic_error(\"Unexpected field mapping request: \" + field);\n    }\n\n    void emit_param(const std::string& value)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"to_tsquery(?)\";\n        m_params.emplace_back(value);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Public facade\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass SearchCompiler\n{\npublic:\n    SqlFragment operator()(std::string_view query)\n    {\n        Parser        parser { query };\n        SqlTranslator translator;\n\n        auto ast = parser.parse();\n        return translator.compile(*ast);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Self-test (only compiled in debug / unit builds)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#ifndef NDEBUG\nstatic void self_test()\n{\n    SearchCompiler compiler;\n    std::vector<std::string> cases = {\n        R\"(security AND (title:\"zero trust\" OR body:firewall))\",\n        R\"(tag:cpp AND NOT author:\"Bad Actor\")\",\n        R\"(backup or disaster AND recovery)\",\n        R\"(\"open source\" AND (license:mit or license:gpl))\" // this will throw (invalid field)\n    };\n\n    for (const auto& q : cases)\n    {\n        try\n        {\n            auto result = compiler(q);\n            std::cout << \"\\nQuery: \" << q << \"\\nSQL:   \" << result.sql << \"\\nParams:\";\n            for (const auto& p : result.params) std::cout << \" [\" << p << \"]\";\n            std::cout << \"\\n\";\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            std::cerr << \"Error compiling query '\" << q << \"': \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct SelfTestRegistrator\n{\n    SelfTestRegistrator() { self_test(); }\n} _registrator;\n\n#endif // NDEBUG\n\n} // namespace IntraLedger::Search\n```","numLines":645,"startLine":1,"totalLines":645}}}

The generator accepts an article body and\n *  a collection of search terms, returning a context-aware excerpt with HTML\n *  <mark> tags around the first few occurrences of each term.\n *\n *  To minimise CPU overhead under heavy query load, results are cached in a\n *  capacity-bounded, thread-safe LRU cache.  The cache key is a combination of\n *  ArticleId and a stable hash of the user’s query string.\n *\n *  Dependencies:\n *    – Standard C++17 library only\n *    – No project-internal headers for -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Basic ASCII-only lower-case conversion; replace with full UTF-8 case folding\n// via ICU or boost::locale in production builds that need it.\ninline std::string ascii_to_lower(std::string_view sv) {\n    std::string out;\n    out.reserve(sv.size());\n    for (unsigned char ch : sv) {\n        out.push_back(static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch)));\n    }\n    return out;\n}\n\n// Trim whitespace (both ends, ASCII definition)\ninline std::string_view trim_ascii(std::string_view sv) {\n    auto begin = sv.find_first_not_of(\" \\t\\r\\n\");\n    if (begin == std::string_view::npos) return {};\n    auto end = sv.find_last_not_of(\" \\t\\r\\n\");\n    return sv.substr(begin, end - begin + 1);\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Thread-safe, capacity-bounded LRU cache\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntemplate <typename Key, typename Value, typename Hash = std::hash<Key>>\nclass ThreadSafeLRUCache final\n{\npublic:\n    explicit ThreadSafeLRUCache(std::size_t capacity)\n        : capacity_{ capacity }\n    {\n        if (capacity_ == 0) {\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"LRU cache capacity must be > 0\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Retrieve a value, or std::nullopt if the key isn’t present.\n    std::optional<Value> get(const Key& key)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock{ mutex_ };\n        auto it = index_.find(key);\n        if (it == index_.end()) return std::nullopt;\n\n        // Move the accessed item to the front of the list to mark it most-recently-used.\n        lru_.splice(lru_.begin(), lru_, it->second);\n        return it->second->second;\n    }\n\n    // Insert or update.\n    void put(Key key, Value value)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock{ mutex_ };\n\n        auto it = index_.find(key);\n        if (it != index_.end()) {\n            // Update existing entry and move to front.\n            it->second->second = std::move(value);\n            lru_.splice(lru_.begin(), lru_, it->second);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Evict if capacity exceeded.\n        if (lru_.size() >= capacity_) {\n            const auto& lru_key = lru_.back().first;\n            index_.erase(lru_key);\n            lru_.pop_back();\n        }\n\n        // Insert new entry at front.\n        lru_.emplace_front(std::move(key), std::move(value));\n        index_[lru_.front().first] = lru_.begin();\n    }\n\n    std::size_t size() const noexcept\n    {\n        std::shared_lock lock{ mutex_ };\n        return lru_.size();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    using List        = std::list<std::pair<Key, Value>>;\n    using ListIt      = typename List::iterator;\n    using Map         = std::unordered_map<Key, ListIt, Hash>;\n\n    std::size_t capacity_;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex mutex_;\n    List lru_;   // Front = MRU, Back = LRU\n    Map  index_;\n};\n\n//        return std::string(body.substr(0, max_len)) + std::string(ellipsis);\n    }\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Cached snippet service std::hash<std::size_t>{}(k.query_hash);\n            // Combine (boost::hash_combine recipe)\n            return h1 ^ (h2 + 0x9e3779b9 + (h1 << 6) + (h1 >> 2));\n        }\n    };\n\n    static Try cache hit (shared lock) -----------------\n        {\n            std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> rLock(m_cacheMutex);\n            auto it = m_cache.find(key);\n            if (it != m_cache.end() && !isExpired(it->second))\n            {\n                return it->second.granted;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---------- 2. Store in cache (unique lock) ----------------\n        {\n            std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n            m_cache[key] = CacheEntry{granted, std::chrono::steady_clock::now()};\n        }\n\n        return granted;\n    }\n\n    /* Explicitly invalidate all cached permissions for a user. */\n    void invalidateUser(std::uint32_t userId)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n        for (auto it = m_cache.begin(); });\n    }\n\n    bool isExpired(const CacheEntry& e) const noexcept\n    {\n        return (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - e.ts) > m_cacheTtl;\n    }\n\n    void housekeepingWorker()\n    {\n        using namespace std::chrono_literals;\n        while (!m_shutdownRequested.load())\n        {\n            {\n                std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n                for (auto it = m_cache.begin(); it != m_cache.end();)\n                {\n                    if (isExpired(it->second))\n                        it = m_cache.erase(it);\n                    else\n                        ++it;\n                }\n            }\n            std::this_thread::sleep_for(15s);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------- Data Members -------------------------- */\n\n    std::shared_ptr<IUserRoleRepository> m_repo;\n    std::chrono::seconds                 m_cacheTtl;\n\n    std::unordered_map<CacheKey, CacheEntry, CacheKeyHasher> m_cache;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex                                  m_cacheMutex;\n\n    std::thread                     m_cleanerThread;\n    std::atomic_bool                m_shutdownRequested{false};\n};\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/* 5. The\n//  service is designed to run inside the built-in asynchronous job processor but can also operate\n//  standalone during integration tests.\n//\n//  High-level flow:\n//     1. }\n\nprivate:\n    void run()\n    {\n        auto nextWakeup = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + pollInterval_;\n        while (!stopFlag_.load(std::memory_order_acquire))\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                processDueArticles();\n            }\n            catch (const std::exception& ex)\n            {\n                log::Logger::error(\"[SchedPub] Fatal error during processing: {}\", ex.what());\n                // Continue looping – we do not want to kill the thread for transient failures\n            }\n\n            // Wait until next interval or stop signal\n            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(cvMutex_);\n            cv_.wait_until(lock, nextWakeup, [this] { return stopFlag_.load(); });\n            nextWakeup = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + pollInterval_;\n        }\n    }\n\n    void processDueArticles()\n    {\n        // 1) Open a new session (RAII – will rollback on destruction if not committed)\n        auto session = sessionFactory_->createSession();\n        domain::repository::ArticleRepository articleRepo{session};\n\n        const auto nowUtc = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n\n        // 2) Fetch eligible articles\n        auto dueArticles = articleRepo.fetchScheduledBefore(nowUtc);\n        if (dueArticles.empty())\n            return;\n\n        log::Logger::debug(\"[SchedPub] {} article(s) ready for publication.\", dueArticles.size());\n\n        // 3) For each article, push a PublishArticleJob\n        for (auto& article : dueArticles)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                // Optimistic locking to avoid double-publishing in race conditions\n                article.setStatus(domain::models::ArticleStatus::Publishing);\n                articleRepo.save(article);\n\n                auto job = std::make_unique<queue::jobs::PublishArticleJob>(article.getId());\n                jobQueue_->push(std::move(job));\n\n                log::Logger::info(\"[SchedPub] Enqueued PublishArticleJob for article #{}\",\n                                  article.getId());\n            }\n            catch (const orm::ConcurrencyException& ex)\n            {\n                // Another worker/thread updated the row first — benign; payload[\"nbf\"].is_number())\n        {\n            if (nowSecs < payload[\"nbf\"].get<int64_t>())\n                throw TokenInvalid(\"Token not yet valid (nbf)\");\n        }\n\n        return std::vector<uint8_t> hmacSHA256(std::string_view data) const\n    {\n        unsigned int len = EVP_MAX_MD_SIZE;\n        std::vector<uint8_t> result(len);\n\n        HMAC_CTX* ctx = HMAC_CTX_new();\n static_cast<int>(cfg_.secretKey.size()),\n                         EVP_sha256(), nullptr) != 1)\n        {\n            HMAC_CTX_free(ctx);\n            throw TokenError(\"HMAC_Init_ex failed\");\n cryptographically secure random hex\n     * string of length `byteCount * 2`.\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    static std::string randomHex(std::size_t byteCount)\n    {\n        std::vector<uint8_t> buf(byteCount);\n        if (RAND_bytes(buf.data(), static_cast<int>(buf.size())) != 1)\n            throw TokenError(\"RAND_bytes failed\");\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        oss << std::hex << std::setfill('0');\n        for (uint8_t b : buf)\n            oss << std::setw(2) << static_cast<int>(b);\n        return oss.str();\n    }\n\n    /*----------------------------------------------------\n     * constantTimeEquals\n     *\n     * Compares two byte sequences in constant time to\n     * mitigate timing attacks.\n     *---------------------------------------------------*/\n    static bool constantTimeEquals(std::span<const uint8_t> a,\n                                   std::span<const uint8_t> b)\n    {\n        if (a.size() != b.size())\n            return false;\n        uint8_t diff = 0;\n        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i)\n            diff |= a[i] ^ b[i];\n        return diff == 0;\n Role-based voter --------------------------------------------------------\nclass RoleVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor /*res*/,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action == \"admin\")\n        {\n            // Only admins may perform \"admin\" actions\n            return roleIn(user, Role::Admin) || roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin);\n        }\n\n        // Non-admin actions are not this voter's responsibility\n        return std::nullopt;\n    }\n};\n\n// 2. Ownership voter ---------------------------------------------------------\nclass OwnershipVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action == \"edit\" || action == \"delete\")\n        {\n            return res.ownerId == user.id || roleIn(user, Role::Editor) || roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin);\n        }\n\n        return std::nullopt;\n    }\n};\n\n// 3. Visibility / subscription voter ----------------------------------------\nclass VisibilityVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action != \"read\")\n            return std::nullopt;\n\n        if (res.isPublic) return true;\n        if (!user.authenticated) return false;\n\n        // Pretend a repository call that certifies premium subscription.\n        // We replace it with simple role check for demo purposes.\n        return res.isPremium ? roleIn(user, Role::Reader) || roleIn(user, Role::Author) ||\n                                   roleIn(user, Role::Editor) || roleIn(user, Role::Admin) ||\n                                   roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin)\n                             : true;\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Permission Service (Affirmative-Based Decision Manager)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass PermissionService\n{\npublic:\n    // Dependency injection (repositories, config, etc.)\n    struct Dependencies\n    {\n        std::shared_ptr<IUserRepository>        userRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository>     articleRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<ISubscriptionRepository> subscriptionRepo;\n    };\n\n    explicit PermissionService(Dependencies deps,\n                               std::size_t    cacheSize = 4'096)\n        : m_deps(std::move(deps))\n        , m_cache(cacheSize)\n    {\n        // Register built-in voters\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<RoleVoter>());\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<OwnershipVoter>());\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<VisibilityVoter>());\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Evaluate permission for a user/action/resource triple.\n     * An \"affirmative\" strategy is used: the first voter that\n     * returns true grants access, while explicit false denies.\n     * If no voter returns a result, deny by default.\n     */\n    bool hasPermission(const UserContext& user,\n                       const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                       std::string_view action)\n    {\n        // Check cache\n        PermissionKey key{user.id, resource.ownerId /* acts as resourceId */, std::string(action)};\n        if (auto cached = m_cache.get(key))\n            return *cached;\n\n        bool result = evaluate(user, resource, action);\n\n        // Store in cache\n        m_cache.put(key, result);\n        return result;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    bool evaluate(const UserContext& user,\n                  const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                  std::string_view action) const\n    {\n        // SuperAdmin shortcut\n        if (user.hasRole(Role::SuperAdmin))\n            return true;\n\n        // Delegating to voters\n        for (const auto& voter : m_voters)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                std::optional<bool> voteResult = voter->vote(user, resource, action);\n\n                if (!voteResult.has_value())\n                    continue; // abstain ‑> next voter\n\n                if (*voteResult)\n                    return true;  // grant immediately\n\n                // deny overrides all\n                return false;\n            }\n            catch (const std::exception& e)\n            {\n                // Defensive: log & move on to next voter\n                std::cerr << \"[security] Voter exception: \" << e.what() << '\\n';\n            }\n        }\n\n        // No voter granted access, deny by default\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    Dependencies                                     m_deps;\n    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IAccessVoter>>       m_voters;\n    LRUCache<PermissionKey, bool, PermissionKeyHasher> m_cache;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Simple inline unit test (can be excluded in production builds)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#ifdef BLOGSUITE_SECURITY_SELFTEST\n#include <cassert>\n\nstatic void selfTest()\n{\n    PermissionService::Dependencies deps{/*nullptr, nullptr, nullptr*/};\n    PermissionService svc(deps, 32);\n\n    UserContext alice { \"alice\", {Role::Author}, true };\n    UserContext bob   { \"bob\",   {Role::Reader}, true };\n    UserContext admin { \"admin\", {Role::Admin},  true };\n\n    ResourceDescriptor pubArticle  { \"alice\", true,  false };\n    ResourceDescriptor privArticle { \"alice\", false, true  };\n\n    // Public read\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(bob, pubArticle, \"read\") );\n\n    // Private read w/o subscription -> deny\n    assert( !svc.hasPermission(bob, privArticle, \"read\") );\n\n    // Owner can edit\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(alice, pubArticle, \"edit\") );\n\n    // Non-owner cannot delete\n    assert( !svc.hasPermission(bob, pubArticle, \"delete\") );\n\n    // Admin can admin\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(admin, pubArticle, \"admin\") );\n\n    // Cache hit (repeat query)\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(admin, pubArticle, \"admin\") );\n}\n\nstatic int _ = (selfTest(), // 128-bit\n        constexpr std::size_t outputLen   = (bytesNeeded * 8 + 5) / 6; // ceil\n\n        std::array<std::uint8_t, bytesNeeded> randomBytes{};\n\n        {\n            static thread_local std::random_device rd;\n            static thread_local std::mt19937_64 rng{rd()};\n            std::uniform_int_distribution<int> dist{0, 255};\n            for (auto& b : randomBytes) b = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(dist(rng));\n        }\n\n        // Convert to base62\n        std::string token;\n        token.reserve(outputLen);\n\n        std::uint64_t accumulator = 0;\n        int bits = 0;\n        for (std::uint8_t byte : randomBytes)\n        {\n            accumulator = (accumulator << 8) | byte;\n            bits += 8;\n            while (bits >= 6)\n            {\n                bits -= 6;\n                token.push_back(charset[(accumulator >> bits) & 0x3F]);\n            }\n        }\n        if (bits > 0)\n            token.push_back(charset[(accumulator << (6 - bits)) & 0x3F]);\n\n        return token;\n    }\n\n    // Rate Limiting --------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    struct RateInfo\n    {\n        std::size_t                           count        = 0;\n        std::chrono::system_clock::time_point lastRequest  = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n    };\n\n    void pruneRateLog(const std::chrono::system_clock::time_point& now)\n    {\n        for (auto it = m_rateLog.begin(); it != m_rateLog.end(); )\n        {\n            if (now - it->second.lastRequest > 1h)\n                it = m_rateLog.erase(it);\n            else\n                ++it;\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    IUserRepository&            m_userRepo;\n    IPasswordResetRepository&   m_tokenRepo;\n    IEmailDispatcher&           m_emailDispatcher;\n    Config                      m_cfg;\n\n    mutable std::mutex                               m_rateMutex;\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, RateInfo>        m_rateLog;\n};\n\n} // namespace intraledger::blogsuite::auth\n```","numLines":291,"startLine":1,"totalLines":291}}}

ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` semantics.\n     */\n    void bulkIncrement(const std::vector<AnalyticsUpdate>& updates)\n    {\n        // NOTE: Implementation of prepared statements is elided.\n        // The call structure is kept to demonstrate expected usage.\n        for (const auto& upd : updates)\n        {\n            (void)upd;\n            // prepare(\"INSERT INTO article_analytics ...\");\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    orm::DbSession& m_session;\n};\n\n}  // namespace repository\n}  // namespace ilb\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Analytics Aggregator – public API (Service Layer)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nnamespace ilb::service\n{\n\n/**\n * Thread-safe, process-wide singleton that collects raw page-view events\n * and flushes the aggregated counters to the persistence layer.\n *\n * Usage:\n *     AnalyticsAggregator::instance().recordView(tenantId, articleId, userId);\n */\nclass AnalyticsAggregator\n{\npublic:\n    // Non-copyable / Non-movable\n    AnalyticsAggregator(const AnalyticsAggregator&)            = delete;\n    AnalyticsAggregator& operator=(const AnalyticsAggregator&) = delete;\n    AnalyticsAggregator(AnalyticsAggregator&&)                 = delete;\n    AnalyticsAggregator& operator=(AnalyticsAggregator&&)      = delete;\n\n    static AnalyticsAggregator& instance()\n    {\n        static AnalyticsAggregator inst;\n        return inst;\n    }\n\n    ~AnalyticsAggregator()\n    {\n        shutdown();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Records a page-view event. Extremely hot path; optimized for minimal\n     * contention via per-bucket sharding and atomic counters.\n     */\n    void recordView(std::int64_t tenantId, std::int64_t articleId, std::int64_t /*userId*/)\n    {\n        const Key key{tenantId, articleId};\n        auto& bucket = m_buckets[key % m_buckets.size()];\n\n        {\n            std::unique_lock lk(bucket.mutex);\n            ++bucket.map[key];\n        }\n\n        // Fast path: only wake the flusher when certain thresholds are met.\n        const auto current = ++m_eventCounter;\n        if (current >= m_flushThreshold)\n        {\n            std::lock_guard lg(m_flushMutex);\n            m_shouldFlush = true;\n            m_cvFlush.notify_one();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Graceful shutdown – ensures all counters make it to persistent storage.\n     * Safe to call multiple times.\n     */\n    void shutdown()\n    {\n        bool expected = false;\n        if (!m_shutdown.compare_exchange_strong(expected, true)) return;\n\n        {\n            std::lock_guard lg(m_flushMutex);\n            m_shouldFlush = true;\n            m_cvFlush.notify_one();\n        }\n        if (m_flushThread.joinable()) m_flushThread.join();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    // ───── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    struct Key\n    {\n        std::int64_t tenantId;\n        std::int64_t articleId;\n\n        std::size_t operator%(std::size_t mod) const noexcept\n        {\n            // Simple mixing hash for bucket selection.\n            std::uint64_t h = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(tenantId) ^ (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(articleId) << 1);\n            return static_cast<std::size_t>(h % mod);\n        }\n\n        bool operator==(const Key& other) const noexcept\n        {\n            return tenantId == other.tenantId && articleId == other.articleId;\n        }\n    };\n\n    struct KeyHash\n    {\n        std::size_t operator()(const Key& k) const noexcept\n        {\n            return k.tenantId * 31ull ^ k.articleId;\n        }\n    };\n\n    using Map  = std::unordered_map<Key, std::uint64_t, KeyHash>;\n    using Pair = std::pair<Key, std::uint64_t>;\n\n    struct Bucket\n    {\n        Map             map;\n        std::mutex      mutex;\n    };\n\n    // ───── Ctor (private) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    AnalyticsAggregator()\n        : m_flushThread(&AnalyticsAggregator::flushLoop, this)\n    {\n    }\n\n    // ───── Flushing Logic ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    void flushLoop()\n    {\n        auto nextWake = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + m_flushInterval;\n        std::unique_lock lk(m_flushMutex);\n\n        while (!m_shutdown.load(std::memory_order_acquire))\n        {\n            m_cvFlush.wait_until(lk, nextWake, [this] { return m_shouldFlush || m_shutdown.load(); });\n            m_shouldFlush = false;\n            lk.unlock();\n\n            try\n            {\n                flushOnce();\n            }\n            catch (const std::exception& ex)\n            {\n                // Log and continue. In production use a proper logger.\n                std::cerr << \"[AnalyticsAggregator] flush failed: \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n            }\n\n            nextWake = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + m_flushInterval;\n            lk.lock();\n        }\n\n        // Final flush on shutdown\n        lk.unlock();\n        flushOnce();\n    }\n\n    void flushOnce()\n    {\n        // Move data out of buckets with minimal holding time.\n        std::vector<Pair> snapshot;\n        snapshot.reserve(m_eventCounter.load());\n\n        for (auto& bucket : m_buckets)\n        {\n            std::lock_guard lg(bucket.mutex);\n            for (auto& [k, v] : bucket.map)\n            {\n                snapshot.emplace_back(k, v);\n            }\n            bucket.map.clear();\n        }\n\n        if (snapshot.empty()) return;\n        m_eventCounter = 0;\n\n        // Aggregate duplicates (rare; only occurs if hash bucket collision across shards).\n        std::unordered_map<Key, std::uint64_t, KeyHash> aggregated;\n        aggregated.reserve(snapshot.size());\n        for (const auto& [k, v] : snapshot) aggregated[k] += v;\n\n        // Persist to DB\n        orm::DbSession session;\n        repository::AnalyticsRepository repo(session);\n\n        session.transactional([&](orm::DbSession& /*tx*/) {\n            std::vector<repository::AnalyticsUpdate> batch;\n            batch.reserve(aggregated.size());\n\n            for (const auto& [k, count] : aggregated)\n            {\n                batch.push_back({k.tenantId, k.articleId, count});\n                if (batch.size() >= m_dbBatchSize)\n                {\n                    repo.bulkIncrement(batch);\n                    batch.clear();\n                }\n            }\n            if (!batch.empty()) repo.bulkIncrement(batch);\n        });\n    }\n\n    // ───── Data Members ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    static constexpr std::size_t kShardCount   = 64;\n    static constexpr std::uint64_t kDefaultThreshold = 10'000;\n\n    std::array<Bucket, kShardCount>    m_buckets{};\n    std::atomic<std::uint64_t>         m_eventCounter{0};\n\n    const std::chrono::seconds         m_flushInterval{10};\n    const std::uint64_t                m_flushThreshold{kDefaultThreshold};\n    const std::size_t                  m_dbBatchSize{1'024};\n\n    std::atomic<bool>                  m_shutdown{false};\n    std::thread                        m_flushThread;\n    std::condition_variable            m_cvFlush;\n    std::mutex                         m_flushMutex;\n    bool                               m_shouldFlush{false};\n};\n\n}  // namespace ilb::service\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Convenience API for other modules (free function wrapper)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nnamespace ilb\n{\n\ninline void recordPageView(std::int64_t tenantId,\n                           std::int64_t articleId,\n                           std::int64_t userId = 0)\n{\n    service::AnalyticsAggregator::instance().recordView(tenantId, articleId, userId);\n}\n\n}  // namespace ilb\n\n// flush.\n}\n#endif\n```","numLines":352,"startLine":1,"totalLines":352}}}

Session Management: PermissionService / UserContext (module_52.cpp)\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_52.cpp\n// Lines: 51-69, 259-302\n\nenum class Role {\n    Reader,\n    Author,\n    Editor,\n    Admin,\n    SuperAdmin\n};\n\nstruct UserContext final\n{\n    std::string            id;\n    std::vector<Role>      roles;\n    bool                   authenticated{false};\n\n    bool hasRole(Role r) const noexcept\n    {\n        return std::find(roles.begin(), roles.end(), r) != roles.end();\n    }\n};\n\nclass PermissionService\n{\npublic:\n    bool hasPermission(const UserContext& user,\n                       const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                       std::string_view action)\n    {\n        // Check cache\n        PermissionKey key{user.id, resource.ownerId, std::string(action)};\n        if (auto cached = m_cache.get(key))\n            return *cached;\n\n        bool result = evaluate(user, resource, action);\n        m_cache.put(key, result);\n        return result;\n    }\n};\n```\n\n### 3. Password Reset Workflow (module_56.cpp)\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_56.cpp\n// Lines: 116-201\n\nclass PasswordResetService final\n{\npublic:\n    void requestPasswordReset(std::string_view email)\n    {\n        validateEmail(email);\n        // Rate limiting check\n        // Generate secure token\n        const auto token = generateToken();\n        // Persist token with expiry\n        m_tokenRepo.upsertToken(token, {\n            std::string{email},\n            now + m_cfg.tokenLifetime\n        });\n        // Dispatch email asynchronously\n        m_emailDispatcher.enqueueResetEmail(email, url.str());\n    }\n\n    void resetPassword(std::string_view token, std::string_view newPasswordHash)\n    {\n        auto record = m_tokenRepo.findToken(token);\n        if (!record)\n            throw TokenInvalid{\"Password-reset token invalid.\"};\n        if (now > record->expiresAt)\n            throw TokenExpired{\"Password-reset token expired.\"};\n\n        m_userRepo.updatePassword(record->email, newPasswordHash);\n        m_tokenRepo.removeToken(token);\n    }\n};\n```\n\n### 4. RBAC Permission Evaluator (module_39.cpp)\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_39.cpp\n// Lines: 134-198\n\nclass PermissionEvaluator\n{\npublic:\n    explicit PermissionEvaluator(std::shared_ptr<IUserRoleRepository> repo,\n                                 std::chrono::seconds cacheTtl = std::chrono::minutes(2))\n\n    bool hasPermission(std::uint32_t userId, Permission perm)\n    {\n        // Cache check (shared lock)\n        // Slow path: fetch from repository\n        std::future<std::vector<Role>> fut =\n            std::async(std::launch::async, &IUserRoleRepository::fetchRolesForUser, m_repo, userId);\n\n        const std::vector<Role> roles = fut.get();\n        granted = evaluateRoles(roles, perm);\n        // Store in cache (unique lock)\n        return granted;\n    }\n\n    void invalidateUser(std::uint32_t userId);\n};\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### 1. Core Component Identification\n\nAfter thorough analysis of the IntraLedger BlogSuite codebase, I have identified the following **primary authentication and session management components**:\n\n#### Primary Authentication Modules:\n\n| Module | Class/Service | Purpose |\n|--------|---------------|---------|\n| **module_74.cpp** | `SecureTokenService` | **Core token management** - JWT-like token generation/validation using HMAC-SHA256 with OpenSSL. Handles all token-based authentication (password resets, email confirmations, OAuth state). |\n| **module_56.cpp** | `PasswordResetService` | **Password reset workflow** - Secure password reset with cryptographic token generation, rate limiting, and email dispatch. |\n| **module_52.cpp** | `PermissionService` | **Authorization gateway** - Central permission evaluation using voter pattern with LRU cache. Defines `UserContext` (the user session representation). |\n\n#### Supporting Authentication Modules:\n\n| Module | Class/Service | Purpose |\n|--------|---------------|---------|\n| **module_39.cpp** | `PermissionEvaluator` | RBAC-based permission evaluation with role membership caching |\n| **module_33.cpp** | `LoginAttemptThrottler` | Login attempt rate limiting and brute-force protection |\n| **module_46.cpp** | `TokenBucketRateLimiter` | Token-bucket rate limiter for login endpoints |\n| **module_11.cpp** | `AdaptiveRateLimiter` | Pluggable rate limiting with Redis/Memcached extension points |\n\n**Note:** The task description mentioned modules 12 and 35 as authentication modules, but analysis reveals these are actually **search modules** (SearchService and related query parsing). The actual authentication core is in modules 74, 56, and 52.\n\n### 2. Dependency Mapping\n\nThe following modules are **directly coupled** to the authentication/session components:\n\n| Dependent Module | Dependency Type | Nature of Coupling |\n|-----------------|-----------------|---------------------|\n| **module_64.cpp** (SearchService) | Uses `UserContext` | Requires `middleware::UserContext` to perform ACL-based filtering of search results via `userCtx.mayReadArticle(hit.articleId)` |\n| **module_66.cpp** (ContentMetricsService) | Uses `UserContext` | Depends on `UserContext.id` and `UserContext.ipAddress` for view deduplication and analytics tracking |\n| **module_76.cpp** (ScheduledPublicationService) | Uses `SessionFactory` | Requires ORM session factory for database transactions (different from user sessions) |\n| **module_39.cpp** (PermissionEvaluator) | Uses `IUserRoleRepository` | Fetches role memberships for permission evaluation, tightly coupled to the authorization subsystem |\n| **module_33.cpp** (LoginAttemptThrottler) | Rate-limits logins | Core authentication infrastructure - tracks failed/successful login attempts |\n| **module_46.cpp** (TokenBucketRateLimiter) | Rate-limits logins | Provides `isLoginAttemptAllowed()` API consumed by authentication middleware |\n| **module_56.cpp** (PasswordResetService) | Uses `IUserRepository`, `IEmailDispatcher` | Depends on user repository for password updates and email dispatcher for reset notifications |\n\n### 3. Key Architectural Observations\n\n1. **UserContext as the Session Representation**: The `UserContext` struct (defined in module_52.cpp and module_66.cpp) serves as the primary session representation, containing user ID, roles, and authentication status.\n\n2. **Token-Based Authentication**: The `SecureTokenService` in module_74.cpp implements JWT-like tokens using HMAC-SHA256, providing the foundation for stateless authentication.\n\n3. **Voter-Based Authorization**: The `PermissionService` uses a voter pattern (similar to Spring Security) where multiple voters (`RoleVoter`, `OwnershipVoter`, `VisibilityVoter`) contribute to access decisions.\n\n4. **Layered Rate Limiting**: Multiple rate limiting implementations exist:\n   - `LoginAttemptThrottler` - Per-user and per-IP rate limiting with database persistence\n   - `TokenBucketRateLimiter` - Process-wide singleton for login rate limiting\n   - `AdaptiveRateLimiter` - Pluggable backend with Redis support\n\n## Decoupling Strategy\n\n### Proposed Architecture: Abstract Authentication Interface (IAuthService)\n\nTo extract the authentication logic into a standalone SSO service while minimizing changes to dependent modules, I propose introducing an **abstract interface** following the **Dependency Inversion Principle**.\n\n#### 1. Define the IAuthService Interface\n\n```cpp\n// File: include/auth/IAuthService.hpp\n\nnamespace intraledger::auth {\n\n// Result types for authentication operations\nstruct AuthResult {\n    bool success;\n    std::optional<UserContext> userContext;\n    std::optional<std::string> errorMessage;\n};\n\nstruct TokenValidationResult {\n    bool valid;\n    std::optional<std::string> userId;\n    std::optional<std::vector<Role>> roles;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point expiresAt;\n};\n\n// Abstract interface for authentication services\nclass IAuthService {\npublic:\n    virtual ~IAuthService() = default;\n\n    // Token operations\n    virtual std::string generateToken(const UserContext& user,\n                                      std::chrono::seconds ttl = std::chrono::seconds{900}) = 0;\n    virtual TokenValidationResult validateToken(std::string_view token) = 0;\n    virtual void revokeToken(std::string_view token) = 0;\n\n    // Session operations\n    virtual std::optional<UserContext> getSession(std::string_view sessionId) = 0;\n    virtual void invalidateSession(std::string_view sessionId) = 0;\n\n    // User authentication\n    virtual AuthResult authenticate(std::string_view username,\n                                    std::string_view passwordHash) = 0;\n\n    // Rate limiting\n    virtual bool isLoginAttemptAllowed(std::string_view clientKey) = 0;\n    virtual void recordLoginFailure(std::string_view clientKey) = 0;\n    virtual void recordLoginSuccess(std::string_view clientKey) = 0;\n};\n\n} // namespace intraledger::auth\n```\n\n#### 2. Create Local Implementation (Current Monolith)\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/auth/LocalAuthService.hpp\n\nclass LocalAuthService final : public IAuthService {\npublic:\n    LocalAuthService(std::shared_ptr<SecureTokenService> tokenService,\n                     std::shared_ptr<PermissionService> permissionService,\n                     std::shared_ptr<LoginAttemptThrottler> throttler)\n        : m_tokenService(std::move(tokenService))\n        , m_permissionService(std::move(permissionService))\n        , m_throttler(std::move(throttler))\n    {}\n\n    // Delegate to existing implementations\n    std::string generateToken(const UserContext& user, std::chrono::seconds ttl) override {\n        nlohmann::json payload = {{\"uid\", user.id}, {\"roles\", serializeRoles(user.roles)}};\n        return m_tokenService->generateToken(payload, ttl);\n    }\n\n    TokenValidationResult validateToken(std::string_view token) override {\n        try {\n            auto payload = m_tokenService->verifyToken(token);\n            return {true, payload[\"uid\"], deserializeRoles(payload[\"roles\"]),\n                    extractExpiry(payload)};\n        } catch (const TokenError&) {\n            return {false, std::nullopt, std::nullopt, {}};\n        }\n    }\n    // ... other method implementations\n};\n```\n\n#### 3. Create Remote SSO Implementation (Future)\n\n```cpp\n// File: src/auth/RemoteSSOService.hpp\n\nclass RemoteSSOService final : public IAuthService {\npublic:\n    RemoteSSOService(std::string ssoEndpoint,\n                     std::shared_ptr<HttpClient> httpClient)\n        : m_ssoEndpoint(std::move(ssoEndpoint))\n        , m_httpClient(std::move(httpClient))\n    {}\n\n    TokenValidationResult validateToken(std::string_view token) override {\n        auto response = m_httpClient->post(\n            m_ssoEndpoint + \"/api/v1/token/validate\",\n            {{\"token\", std::string(token)}}\n        );\n\n        if (response.status == 200) {\n            auto json = nlohmann::json::parse(response.body);\n            return {true, json[\"userId\"], parseRoles(json[\"roles\"]),\n                    parseExpiry(json[\"expiresAt\"])};\n        }\n        return {false, std::nullopt, std::nullopt, {}};\n    }\n    // ... other method implementations\n};\n```\n\n#### 4. Update Dependent Modules\n\nRefactor dependent modules to use the `IAuthService` interface:\n\n```cpp\n// Before (module_64.cpp - SearchService)\nclass SearchService {\npublic:\n    std::vector<SearchResult> search(std::string_view query,\n                                     const middleware::UserContext& userCtx,\n                                     std::size_t limit) const {\n        // Direct access to UserContext\n        if (!userCtx.mayReadArticle(hit.articleId)) { continue; }\n    }\n};\n\n// After\nclass SearchService {\npublic:\n    SearchService(std::shared_ptr<IAuthService> authService,\n                  std::shared_ptr<persistence::ArticleRepository> repo,\n                  std::shared_ptr<search::ISearchEngine> engine)\n        : m_authService(std::move(authService))\n        // ...\n    {}\n\n    std::vector<SearchResult> search(std::string_view query,\n                                     std::string_view sessionToken,\n                                     std::size_t limit) const {\n        auto validationResult = m_authService->validateToken(sessionToken);\n        if (!validationResult.valid) {\n            throw UnauthorizedException{};\n        }\n\n        // Create UserContext from token claims\n        UserContext userCtx{validationResult.userId.value(),\n                           validationResult.roles.value_or({}), true};\n\n        // Continue with existing logic\n    }\n};\n```\n\n### Migration Strategy\n\n1. **Phase 1 - Interface Introduction**: Add `IAuthService` interface and `LocalAuthService` implementation that wraps existing modules. No behavioral changes.\n\n2. **Phase 2 - Dependency Injection**: Update all dependent modules to accept `IAuthService` via constructor injection instead of direct dependencies.\n\n3. **Phase 3 - Configuration-Based Selection**: Implement factory/DI container configuration to select between `LocalAuthService` and `RemoteSSOService` based on environment.\n\n4. **Phase 4 - SSO Service Extraction**: Deploy the standalone SSO service and switch production configuration to use `RemoteSSOService`.\n\n### Benefits of This Approach\n\n1. **Dependency Inversion**: Application depends on abstraction (`IAuthService`), not concrete implementations\n2. **Minimal Code Changes**: Existing modules require only constructor signature changes\n3. **Testability**: Easy to mock `IAuthService` for unit testing\n4. **Gradual Migration**: Can switch implementations per-environment without code changes\n5. **Backward Compatibility**: `LocalAuthService` preserves existing behavior during transition\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The core authentication system consists of **module_74.cpp (SecureTokenService)**, **module_56.cpp (PasswordResetService)**, and **module_52.cpp (PermissionService)** - not modules 12/35 as initially suggested (those are search modules).\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - `SecureTokenService` - JWT-like token generation/validation with HMAC-SHA256\n  - `PermissionService` - Voter-based authorization with LRU caching\n  - `PasswordResetService` - Secure password reset workflow\n  - `LoginAttemptThrottler` / `TokenBucketRateLimiter` - Rate limiting infrastructure\n  - `UserContext` - Session representation containing user ID, roles, and auth status\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The recommended decoupling strategy uses the **Dependency Inversion Principle** with an **abstract IAuthService interface**, allowing transparent switching between local implementation and remote SSO service.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Introduce `IAuthService` interface as the authentication contract\n  2. Create `LocalAuthService` wrapper for existing implementations\n  3. Update dependent modules to use dependency injection\n  4. **RBAC PermissionEvaluator**: Role-based access control permission evaluator with TTL-based caching\n- `/app/project/src/module_33.cpp` - **LoginAttemptThrottler**: Rate-limiting for login attempts to prevent brute-force attacks\n- `/app/project/src/module_46.cpp` - **SearchService**: Depends on `middleware::UserContext` for ACL-based filtering\n- `/app/project/src/module_66.cpp` - **ContentMetricsService**: Uses `UserContext` for view deduplication\n- `/app/project/src/module_76.cpp` -
